- **ast-grep**: Structural code search and rewriting with ast-grep, including how to write its rules. Use the moment you are about to put a newline, \s+, or a brace into a regex that matches code; when the same replacement has to be written again because only an identifier differs between the sites; when a match depends on nesting, such as inside a function, a struct literal, or a test body; or when asked for an AST-based search. Prefer it over multi-line sd, perl, or sed regexes in those cases. Plain rg stays right for locating a symbol to read or edit. ([ambi/dotfiles](https://github.com/ambi/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/home/dot_agents/skills/ast-grep))
